8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>1. Run your first job</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Run a simple Python script</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;print(&quot;Hello from HF compute!&quot;)&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>This command runs the job and shows the logs. You can pass <code>--detach</code> to run the Job in the background and only print the Job ID.</p> <!--[3--><h4 class="relative group"><a id="2-check-job-status" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#2-check-job-status"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>2. Check job status</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># List your running jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span>
<span class="hljs-comment"># List all jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -a

<span class="hljs-comment"># Inspect the status of a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> inspect &lt;job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># View logs from a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> logs &lt;job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># View resources usage stats and metrics of running jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> stats
<span class="hljs-comment"># View resources usage stats and metrics of some jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> stats [job_ids]...

<span class="hljs-comment"># Cancel a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> cancel &lt;job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># Wait until one or more jobs finish (exit code 0 only if all jobs completed successfully)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">wait</span> &lt;job_id&gt; [&lt;job_id&gt;...]

<span class="hljs-comment"># Wait for all currently running jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -q | xargs hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">wait</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Non-detached <code>hf jobs run</code> and <code>hf jobs wait</code> exit with a non-zero code if a Job fails, so you can chain commands with <code>&amp;&amp;</code>:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run python:3.12 python train.py &amp;&amp; <span class="hljs-built_in">echo</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;training succeeded&quot;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[3--><h4 class="relative group"><a id="3-run-on-gpu" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#3-run-on-gpu"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>3. Run on GPU</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <p>You can also run jobs on GPUs or TPUs with the <code>--flavor</code> option. For example, to run a PyTorch job on an A10G GPU:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Use an A10G GPU to check PyTorch CUDA</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --flavor a10g-small pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel \
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;import torch; print(f&quot;This code ran with the following GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name()}&quot;)&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Running this will show the following output!</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!---->This code ran with the following GPU: NVIDIA A10G<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use <code>--</code> to separate Jobs options from arguments passed to your command or script.
Jobs does not interpret options after <code>--</code>.</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --flavor cpu-basic python:3.12 -- python --<span class="hljs-built_in">help</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>That’s it! You’re now running code on Hugging Face’s infrastructure.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="common-use-cases" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#common-use-cases"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Common Use Cases</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <ul><li><strong>Model Training</strong>: Fine-tune or train models on GPUs (T4, A10G, A100) without managing infrastructure</li> <li><strong>Synthetic Data Generation</strong>: Generate large-scale datasets using LLMs on powerful hardware</li> <li><strong>Data Processing</strong>: Process massive datasets with high-CPU configurations for parallel workloads</li> <li><strong>Batch Inference</strong>: Run offline inference on thousands of samples using optimized GPU setups</li> <li><strong>Experiments &amp; Benchmarks</strong>: Run ML experiments on consistent hardware for reproducible results</li> <li><strong>Development &amp; Debugging</strong>: Test GPU code without local CUDA setup</li></ul> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="pass-environment-variables-and-secrets" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#pass-environment-variables-and-secrets"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Pass Environment variables and Secrets</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>You can pass environment variables to your job using</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Pass environment variables</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run -e FOO=foo -e BAR=bar python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;import os; print(os.environ[&quot;FOO&quot;], os.environ[&quot;BAR&quot;])&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Pass an environment from a local .env file</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --env-file .<span class="hljs-built_in">env</span> python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;import os; print(os.environ[&quot;FOO&quot;], os.environ[&quot;BAR&quot;])&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Pass secrets - they will be encrypted server side</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run -s MY_SECRET=psswrd python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;import os; print(os.environ[&quot;MY_SECRET&quot;])&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Pass secrets from a local .env.secrets file - they will be encrypted server side</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --secrets-file .env.secrets python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;import os; print(os.environ[&quot;MY_SECRET&quot;])&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <blockquote class="tip"><p>Use <code>--secrets HF_TOKEN</code> to pass your local Hugging Face token implicitly.
With this syntax, the secret is retrieved from the environment variable.
For <code>HF_TOKEN</code>, it may read the token file located in the Hugging Face home folder if the environment variable is unset.</p></blockquote> <!--[3--><h4 class="relative group"><a id="built-in-environment-variables" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#built-in-environment-variables"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Built-in Environment Variables</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <p>Inside the job container, the following environment variables are automatically available:</p> <table><thead><tr><th>Variable</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>JOB_ID</code></td><td>The unique identifier of the current job. Use this to reference the job programmatically.</td></tr><tr><td><code>ACCELERATOR</code></td><td>The type of accelerator available (e.g., <code>t4-medium</code>, <code>a10g-small</code>, <code>a100x4</code>). Empty if no accelerator.</td></tr><tr><td><code>CPU_CORES</code></td><td>The number of CPU cores available to the job (e.g., <code>2</code>, <code>4</code>, <code>8</code>).</td></tr><tr><td><code>MEMORY</code></td><td>The amount of memory available to the job (e.g., <code>16Gi</code>, <code>32Gi</code>).</td></tr></tbody></table> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Access job environment information</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import os; print(f&#x27;Job: {os.environ.get(\&quot;JOB_ID\&quot;)}, CPU: {os.environ.get(\&quot;CPU_CORES\&quot;)}, Mem: {os.environ.get(\&quot;MEMORY\&quot;)}&#x27;)&quot;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="job-timeout" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#job-timeout"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Job Timeout</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Jobs have a default timeout of 30 mins, after which they automatically stop. For long-running tasks like model training, set a custom timeout using the <code>--timeout</code> option:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Set timeout in seconds (default unit)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --<span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span> 7200 python:3.12 python train.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Use time units: s (seconds), m (minutes), h (hours), d (days)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --<span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span> 2h pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel python train.py
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --<span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span> 90m python:3.12 python process_data.py
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --<span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span> 1.5h python:3.12 python train.py  <span class="hljs-comment"># floats are supported</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>The <code>--timeout</code> option also works with UV scripts and scheduled jobs:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># UV script with timeout</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --<span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span> 2h training_script.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Scheduled job with timeout</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled run @daily --<span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span> 4h python:3.12 python daily_task.py<!----></pre></div><!----> <blockquote class="warning"><p>If your job exceeds the timeout, it will be automatically terminated. Always set an appropriate timeout with some buffer for long-running tasks to avoid unexpected job terminations.</p></blockquote> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="hardware" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#hardware"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Hardware</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Available <code>--flavor</code> options:</p> <ul><li>CPU: <code>cpu-basic</code>, <code>cpu-upgrade</code></li> <li>GPU: <code>t4-small</code>, <code>t4-medium</code>, <code>l4x1</code>, <code>l4x4</code>, <code>a10g-small</code>, <code>a10g-large</code>, <code>a10g-largex2</code>, <code>a10g-largex4</code>,<code>a100-large</code></li> <li>TPU: <code>v5e-1x1</code>, <code>v5e-2x2</code>, <code>v5e-2x4</code></li></ul> <p>(updated in 07/2025 from Hugging Face <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/hub/en/spaces-config-reference" rel="nofollow">suggested_hardware docs</a>)</p> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="volumes" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#volumes"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Volumes</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Mount a volume on the Job’s disk using <code>-v</code> or <code>--volume</code>.</p> <p>You can mount any Hugging Face Repository (model/dataset/space) or <a href="/docs/hub/storage-buckets">Storage Bucket</a> using the <code>hf://</code> URL scheme. For example:</p> <ul><li>mount a model repository: <code>-v hf://openai/gpt-oss-120b:/model</code></li> <li>mount a dataset repository: <code>-v hf://datasets/HuggingFaceFW/fineweb:/data</code></li> <li>mount a storage bucket: <code>-v hf://buckets/username/my-bucket:/mnt</code></li> <li>mount a space: <code>-v hf://spaces/username/my-space:/app</code></li> <li>mount a subfolder inside a repo: <code>-v hf://datasets/org/ds/train:/data</code></li></ul> <p>Then you can use the mounted volume as a local directory:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Docker Job with a mounted volume as input</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run -v hf://datasets/HuggingFaceFW/fineweb:/dataset \
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    duckdb/duckdb duckdb -c <span class="hljs-string">&quot;SELECT * FROM &#x27;/dataset/**/*.parquet&#x27; LIMIT 5&quot;</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># UV Job with a mounted volume to save checkpoints when training a model</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run -v hf://buckets/username/my-bucket:/training-outputs \
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>    sft.py --output-dir /training-outputs/training-v3-final ...<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Models, datasets and spaces are always mounted read-only. Storage buckets are read+write by default — this is especially useful for data that changes frequently, as files can be overwritten or deleted in place.</p> <p>Use <code>:ro</code> to enable read-only:</p> <ul><li>mount a storage bucket in read-only: <code>-v hf://buckets/username/my-bucket:/mnt:ro</code></li></ul> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="mount-local-data" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#mount-local-data"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Mount local data</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>The source side of <code>-v</code> can also be a local directory. It is first synced to your <code>jobs-artifacts</code> <a href="/docs/hub/storage-buckets">Storage Bucket</a> and the resulting bucket folder is mounted in the Job:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run -v ./my-data:/data process.py<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Re-running the command only uploads new or modified files. Local directories are mounted read-only by default. Use <code>:rw</code> to let the Job write to the volume, e.g. to retrieve outputs after the Job completes (an empty local directory works too):</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run -v ./pdfs:/input -v ./md-out:/output:rw ocr.py
...
Hint: Volume <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;/output&#x27;</span> is mounted read-write. Once the job is over, pull back its data with:
  hf buckets <span class="hljs-built_in">sync</span> hf://buckets/username/jobs-artifacts/md-out-a1b2c3d4 ./md-out<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="labels" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#labels"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Labels</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Add labels to a Job using <code>-l</code> or <code>--label</code>. Labels are a key=value pairs that applies metadata to a Job. To label a Job with two labels, repeat the label flag (<code>-l</code> or <code>--label</code>):</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run -l my-label --label foo=bar ubuntu <span class="hljs-built_in">echo</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;This Job has multiple labels&quot;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>The my-label key doesn’t specify a value so its value defaults to an empty string ("").</p> <p>Use <code>--name</code> to add the <code>name</code> label when creating a Job. Names make Jobs easier to find and identify in the UI; they are optional and do not have to be unique. If you don’t pass <code>--name</code>, a name is derived automatically from the Docker image or the script, plus a short hash of the command so reruns of the same command share a name (e.g. <code>python:3.12 foo --truc</code> → <code>python-3-12-1a2b3c4d</code>). You can also rename an existing Job:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --name training-v2 python:3.12 python train.py
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> labels &lt;job_id&gt; --name training-v2<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use <code>--status</code>, <code>--label</code> and <code>--name</code> in <code>hf jobs ls</code> to filter Jobs. <code>--status</code> takes one or more statuses, <code>--label</code> takes <code>key=value</code> pairs, and <code>--name</code> is a shortcut for <code>--label name=NAME</code>. A Job must match every filter to be listed. The Job name is also shown as its own <code>NAME</code> column:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Show completed Jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -a --status completed

<span class="hljs-comment"># Show running or scheduling Jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> --status running,scheduling

<span class="hljs-comment"># Show Jobs named `training-v2`</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -a --name training-v2

<span class="hljs-comment"># Show Jobs with the `model=Qwen3-06B` label</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -a --label model=Qwen3-06B

<span class="hljs-comment"># Combine filters: running Jobs labelled both `env=prod` and `team=ml`</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> --status running --label <span class="hljs-built_in">env</span>=prod --label team=ml<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>By default <code>hf jobs ps</code> displays at most 100 Jobs to avoid bloating the terminal. Use <code>--limit</code> to change this, or <code>--limit 0</code> to show all of them:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Show up to 500 Jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> ps -a --<span class="hljs-built_in">limit</span> 500

<span class="hljs-comment"># Show all Jobs (no limit)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> ps -a --<span class="hljs-built_in">limit</span> 0<!----></pre></div><!----> <blockquote class="warning"><p><code>-f</code>/<code>--filter</code> is deprecated in favor of <code>--status</code> and <code>--label</code>. Matching is exact: glob patterns (<code>data-*</code>) and negation (<code>key!=value</code>) are not supported, and filtering by <code>id</code>, <code>image</code> or <code>command</code> is not available.</p></blockquote> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="ssh-into-a-job" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#ssh-into-a-job"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>SSH into a Job</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Pass <code>--ssh</code> to <code>hf jobs run</code> (or <code>hf jobs uv run</code>) to make the Job’s container reachable over SSH, then connect with <code>hf jobs ssh</code>:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Start a job with SSH enabled</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> run --ssh --detach python:3.12 <span class="hljs-built_in">sleep</span> infinity

<span class="hljs-comment"># Open an SSH session into it</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> ssh &lt;job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># Print the SSH command instead of running it</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> ssh &lt;job_id&gt; --dry-run

<span class="hljs-comment"># Use a specific identity file</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> ssh &lt;job_id&gt; -i ~/.ssh/id_ed25519<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Only users with write access to the Job’s namespace are allowed in (the Job creator, or members of the owner organization), authenticated by an SSH public key registered at <a href="https://huggingface.co/settings/keys" rel="nofollow">https://huggingface.co/settings/keys</a>.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="uv-scripts-experimental" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#uv-scripts-experimental"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>UV Scripts (Experimental)</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Run UV scripts (Python scripts with inline dependencies) on HF infrastructure. UV scripts are Python scripts that include their dependencies directly in the file using a special comment syntax.</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Run a UV script (creates temporary repo)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run my_script.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run with persistent repo</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --repo my-uv-scripts my_script.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run with GPU</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --flavor t4-small ml_training.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Pass arguments to script</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run process.py input.csv output.parquet

<span class="hljs-comment"># Add dependencies</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --with transformers --with torch train.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run a script directly from a URL</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run https://huggingface.co/datasets/username/scripts/resolve/main/example.py

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run a command</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --with lighteval python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;import lighteval&#x27;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <p>UV scripts are Python scripts that include their dependencies directly in the file using a special comment syntax. This makes them perfect for self-contained tasks that don’t require complex project setups. Learn more about UV scripts in the <a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/guides/scripts/" rel="nofollow">UV documentation</a>.</p> <p>Use <code>--</code> to pass conflicting options through to your script. Here, <code>--help</code> reaches <code>train.py</code> rather than showing Jobs help:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> uv run --flavor t4-small train.py -- --<span class="hljs-built_in">help</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="hf-jobs-scheduled" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#hf-jobs-scheduled"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>hf jobs scheduled</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Schedule and manage jobs that will run on HF infrastructure.</p> <p>The schedule should be one of <code>@annually</code>, <code>@yearly</code>, <code>@monthly</code>, <code>@weekly</code>, <code>@daily</code>, <code>@hourly</code>, or a CRON schedule expression (e.g., <code>"0 9 * * 1"</code> for 9 AM every Monday).</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Schedule a job that runs every hour</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled run @hourly --name hourly-task python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;print(&quot;This runs every hour!&quot;)&#x27;</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># Use the CRON syntax</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled run <span class="hljs-string">&quot;*/5 * * * *&quot;</span> python:3.12 python -c <span class="hljs-string">&#x27;print(&quot;This runs every 5 minutes!&quot;)&#x27;</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># Schedule with GPU</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled run @hourly --flavor a10g-small pytorch/pytorch:2.6.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-devel \
<span class="hljs-meta">... </span>python -c <span class="hljs-string">&quot;import torch; print(f&quot;</span>This code ran with the following GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name()}<span class="hljs-string">&quot;)&quot;</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># Schedule a UV script</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled uv run @hourly --name hourly-script my_script.py<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use the same parameters as <code>hf jobs run</code> to pass environment variables, secrets, timeout, etc.</p> <p>Manage scheduled jobs using</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># List your active scheduled jobs</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># Same filters as `hf jobs ls`: --status, --label and --name</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> --all
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> --status suspended
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> --name hourly-task --label <span class="hljs-built_in">env</span>=prod

<span class="hljs-comment"># Inspect the status of a job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled inspect &lt;scheduled_job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># Suspend (pause) a scheduled job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled <span class="hljs-built_in">suspend</span> &lt;scheduled_job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># Resume a scheduled job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled resume &lt;scheduled_job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># Trigger a scheduled job to run right now (does not change the schedule)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled trigger &lt;scheduled_job_id&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment"># Delete a scheduled job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf <span class="hljs-built_in">jobs</span> scheduled delete &lt;scheduled_job_id&gt;<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="hf-sandbox" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#hf-sandbox"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>hf sandbox</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p><code>hf sandbox</code> spins up isolated cloud machines built on Jobs: create one, run commands with live-streamed output, and copy files in and out. Any Docker image with <code>/bin/sh</code> works. See the <a href="./sandbox">Sandboxes guide</a> for the Python API, and the <a href="../concepts/sandbox">conceptual guide</a> for how it works under the hood.</p> <blockquote class="note"><p>Sandboxes are experimental, and their API and behavior may change without notice. Shared sandboxes are intended for
workloads within the same trust boundary; use a dedicated sandbox for workloads that do not trust each other.</p></blockquote> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Create a sandbox (waits until it is ready, prints its id)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf sandbox create
✓ Sandbox ready <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>=687f911eaea852de79c4a50a image=python:3.12 elapsed=6.0s

<span class="hljs-comment"># Attach labels to the underlying Job</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf sandbox create --label controller-run=run-42 --label team=data-infra

<span class="hljs-comment"># Run commands inside it (output is streamed, exit code is propagated)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf sandbox <span class="hljs-built_in">exec</span> 687f911eaea852de79c4a50a -- python -c <span class="hljs-string">&quot;print(&#x27;hi&#x27;)&quot;</span>
hi

<span class="hljs-comment"># Copy files in and out (docker-style)</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf sandbox <span class="hljs-built_in">cp</span> data.csv 687f911eaea852de79c4a50a:/data/data.csv
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf sandbox <span class="hljs-built_in">cp</span> 687f911eaea852de79c4a50a:/app/results.json results.json

<span class="hljs-comment"># Terminate a sandbox</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf sandbox <span class="hljs-built_in">kill</span> 687f911eaea852de79c4a50a<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use <code>--flavor</code> to pick hardware (e.g. <code>a10g-small</code>), <code>--idle-timeout</code> to bound the sandbox lifetime, <code>-l</code> / <code>--label</code> to attach labels to its Job, and <code>-e</code> / <code>--secrets</code> for environment variables. To fan out many cheap CPU sandboxes, warm a pool with <code>hf sandbox pool create</code> and spawn into it with <code>hf sandbox create --pool &lt;id></code> (see the <a href="./sandbox#from-the-cli">Sandboxes guide</a>).</p> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="hf-webhooks" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#hf-webhooks"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>hf webhooks</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p><code>hf webhooks</code> lets you manage webhooks on the Hugging Face Hub directly from the terminal. Webhooks allow you to listen for events (pushes, discussions, etc.) on repos, users, or organizations and trigger actions — either by pinging a remote URL or by running a Job on Hugging Face infrastructure.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="list-webhooks" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 ID           URL                                DISABLED  DOMAINS  WATCHED
wh-abc123    https://example.com/hook            False     repo     model:bert-base-uncased
wh-def456    https://example.com/other-hook      False     repo     org:HuggingFace<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Use <code>--format json</code> for machine-readable output, or <code>-q</code> to print only IDs:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> --format json
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span> -q<!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="get-webhook-info" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#get-webhook-info"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true"
