class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks info wh-abc123<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Prints full webhook details as JSON.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="create-a-webhook" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#create-a-webhook"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Create a webhook</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Create a webhook that sends payloads to a URL:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks create --url https://example.com/hook --watch model:bert-base-uncased
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks create --url https://example.com/hook --watch org:HuggingFace --watch model:gpt2 --domain repo<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Or create a webhook that triggers a Job instead:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks create --job-id 687f911eaea852de79c4a50a --watch user:julien-c<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>The <code>--watch</code> option uses the format <code>type:name</code> where type is one of <code>model</code>, <code>dataset</code>, <code>space</code>, <code>org</code>, or <code>user</code>. It can be repeated to watch multiple items. Use <code>--domain</code> to filter events to <code>repo</code> or <code>discussions</code>, and <code>--secret</code> to set a signing secret.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="update-a-webhook" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#update-a-webhook"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks update wh-abc123 --url https://new-url.com/hook
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf webhooks update wh-abc123 --watch model:gpt2 --domain repo<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>Only the provided options are changed. Use <code>--help</code> on any command to see all options.</p></blockquote> <!--[1--><h2 class="relative group"><a id="hf-endpoints" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#hf-endpoints"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>hf endpoints</span></h2><!--]--><!----> <p>Use <code>hf endpoints</code> to list, deploy, describe, and manage Inference Endpoints directly from the terminal. The legacy <code>hf inference-endpoints</code> alias remains available for compatibility.</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># Lists endpoints in your namespace</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># List the hardware you can deploy on</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints hardware

<span class="hljs-comment"># Deploy an endpoint from Model Catalog</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints catalog deploy --repo openai/gpt-oss-120b --name my-endpoint

<span class="hljs-comment"># Deploy an endpoint from the Hugging Face Hub</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints deploy my-endpoint --repo gpt2 --framework pytorch --accelerator cpu --instance-size x2 --instance-type intel-icl

<span class="hljs-comment"># List catalog entries</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints catalog <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># Show status and metadata</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints describe my-endpoint

<span class="hljs-comment"># Pause the endpoint</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints pause my-endpoint

<span class="hljs-comment"># Delete without confirmation prompt</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints delete my-endpoint --<span class="hljs-built_in">yes</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <blockquote class="tip"><p>Add <code>--namespace</code> to target an organization, <code>--token</code> to override authentication.</p></blockquote> <!--[3--><h4 class="relative group"><a id="find-hardware-to-deploy-on" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#find-hardware-to-deploy-on"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Find hardware to deploy on</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <p><code>hf endpoints deploy</code> needs five hardware flags (<code>--vendor</code>, <code>--region</code>, <code>--accelerator</code>, <code>--instance-type</code> and <code>--instance-size</code>). <code>hf endpoints hardware</code> lists the valid combinations, with the price per replica per hour in USD and the accelerator quota of your namespace:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints hardware --vendor aws --region eu-west-1
VENDOR REGION    ACCELERATOR INSTANCE_TYPE INSTANCE_SIZE MEMORY_GB GPU_MEMORY_GB PRICE_PER_HOUR QUOTA STATUS
------ --------- ----------- ------------- ------------- --------- ------------- -------------- ----- ---------
aws    eu-west-1 cpu         intel-spr     x1                  2.0                        0.033 0/60  available
aws    eu-west-1 cpu         intel-spr     x2                  4.0                        0.067 0/60  available
aws    eu-west-1 cpu         intel-spr     x4                  8.0                        0.134 0/60  available
aws    eu-west-1 cpu         intel-spr     x8                 16.0                        0.268 0/60  available
aws    eu-west-1 cpu         intel-spr     x16                32.0                        0.536 0/60  available
aws    eu-west-1 gpu         nvidia-a10g   x1                 30.0            24            1.0 0/16  available
aws    eu-west-1 gpu         nvidia-t4     x1                 15.0            16            0.5 1/30  available
Hint: Deploy on one of these, e.g.: hf endpoints deploy my-endpoint --repo &lt;repo&gt; --framework &lt;framework&gt; --vendor aws --region eu-west-1 --accelerator cpu --instance-type intel-spr --instance-size x1<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>The filter flags are the deploy flags (<code>--vendor</code>, <code>--region</code>, <code>--accelerator</code>, <code>--instance-type</code>), so whatever you filter on is what you pass to <code>deploy</code>. Only hardware you can deploy on right now is listed: a usable status, and enough accelerator quota left in your namespace for one replica. Add <code>--all</code> to also see what is deprecated, temporarily unavailable or out of quota. <code>--format json</code> adds the remaining per-replica specs (vCPUs, architecture, number of accelerators) to each entry.</p> <blockquote class="tip"><p>Quota is per namespace, and it decides which rows are listed at all. If you are deploying into an organization, pass the same <code>--namespace</code> you will pass to <code>deploy</code> — otherwise you are looking at your personal quota, which can both hide hardware the organization can deploy on and show hardware it cannot.</p></blockquote> <!--[3--><h4 class="relative group"><a id="deploy-a-custom-container" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#deploy-a-custom-container"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Deploy a custom container</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <p>To deploy your own Docker image instead of a Hugging Face managed one, pass <code>--framework custom</code> together with <code>--custom-image</code>. The model repository is mounted at <code>/repository</code> inside the container. Use <code>--container-args</code> (and optionally <code>--container-command</code>) to pass a quoted launch string, <code>--env</code>/<code>--secrets</code> to inject environment variables, and <code>--type</code> to set the access type (<code>public</code>, <code>authenticated</code> or <code>private</code>):</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints deploy nex-n2-pro \
      --repo nex-agi/Nex-N2-Pro \
      --framework custom \
      --accelerator gpu --vendor aws --region us-east-1 \
      --instance-type nvidia-h200 --instance-size x8 \
      --custom-image nexagi/sglang:v0.5.12 \
      --health-route /health --port 30000 \
      --container-args <span class="hljs-string">&quot;--reasoning-parser qwen3 --tool-call-parser qwen3_coder --mamba-scheduler-strategy extra_buffer --tp 8&quot;</span> \
      --<span class="hljs-built_in">env</span> MODEL_ID=/repository \
      --<span class="hljs-built_in">type</span> authenticated<!----></pre></div><!----> <p><code>--container-args</code> and <code>--container-command</code> are not limited to custom images: they map to <code>model.args</code> and <code>model.command</code> in the API payload, which apply to managed images as well. On an existing endpoint both flags replace the current value rather than adding to it, so pass the full list you want and use an empty string to clear it. Endpoints deployed from the catalog already come with tuned engine flags, so check <code>hf endpoints describe</code> before overwriting them:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints update my-endpoint \
      --container-args <span class="hljs-string">&quot;--enable-auto-tool-choice --tool-call-parser lfm2&quot;</span><!----></pre></div><!----> <!--[3--><h4 class="relative group"><a id="deploy-a-managed-engine-image" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#deploy-a-managed-engine-image"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>Deploy a managed engine image</span></h4><!--]--><!----> <p><code>--custom-image</code> alone deploys an arbitrary container. Add <code>--engine</code> to run it as one of the engines the API manages (<code>vllm</code>, <code>sglang</code>, <code>tgi</code>, <code>tei</code>, <code>llamacpp</code>, <code>hf-serve</code>, …), which unlocks that engine’s settings, including <code>--tensor-parallel-size</code> and <code>--data-parallel-size</code>. vLLM and SGLang default to one accelerator while the endpoint gets every accelerator of its instance, so leaving both unset would load the model onto one and idle the rest while still reporting healthy, which is why the API now rejects that configuration:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints deploy gpt-oss-120b-vllm \
      --repo openai/gpt-oss-120b \
      --framework custom \
      --accelerator gpu --vendor aws --region us-east-1 \
      --instance-type nvidia-h200 --instance-size x8 \
      --engine vllm --custom-image vllm/vllm-openai:v0.23.0 \
      --tensor-parallel-size 8<!----></pre></div><!----> <p>This is not the same as <code>--container-args "... --tp 8"</code> above: <code>--tensor-parallel-size</code> writes into the engine’s <code>model.image</code> config, which is what the API validates against the instance’s accelerator count, while <code>--container-args</code> only appends a flag to the command line. Use <code>--container-args</code> for engine flags with no image field, and for plain custom containers, which have no parallelism fields.</p> <p>To retune a running endpoint, pass the sizes on their own. <code>model.image</code> is sent as a whole and requires <code>url</code>, so the endpoint’s current image is fetched and updated in place:</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints update gpt-oss-120b-vllm --tensor-parallel-size 4 --data-parallel-size 2<!----></pre></div><!----> <p><code>hf endpoints update</code> also takes <code>--custom-image</code> and <code>--engine</code>, the only way to change an endpoint’s image from the CLI. That path replaces <code>model.image</code> instead of patching it, so run <code>hf endpoints describe</code> first and pass back what you want to keep.</p> <!--[2--><h3 class="relative group"><a id="hf-endpoints-catalog" class="header-link block pr-1.5 text-lg no-hover:hidden with-hover:absolute with-hover:p-1.5 with-hover:opacity-0 with-hover:group-hover:opacity-100 with-hover:right-full" href="#hf-endpoints-catalog"><span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><!----></span></a> <span>hf endpoints catalog</span></h3><!--]--><!----> <p>Use <code>hf endpoints catalog</code> to interact with the Inference Endpoints Model Catalog. Deploy models directly from the catalog with optimized configurations.</p> <div class="code-block relative "><div class="absolute top-2.5 right-4"><button class="inline-flex items-center relative text-sm focus:text-green-500 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none transition duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 mx-0.5   text-gray-600  " title="code excerpt" type="button"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M28,10V28H10V10H28m0-2H10a2,2,0,0,0-2,2V28a2,2,0,0,0,2,2H28a2,2,0,0,0,2-2V10a2,2,0,0,0-2-2Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><path d="M4,18H2V4A2,2,0,0,1,4,2H18V4H4Z" transform="translate(0)"></path><rect fill="none" width="32" height="32"></rect></svg><!----> <div class=" absolute pointer-events-none transition-opacity bg-black text-white py-1 px-2 leading-tight rounded font-normal shadow left-1/2 top-full transform -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-2 opacity-0 "><div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 w-0 h-0 border-black border-4 border-t-0" style="border-left-color: transparent; border-right-color: transparent;"></div> Copied</div><!----></button><!----></div> <pre class="language-bash "><!----><span class="hljs-comment"># List available catalog models</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints catalog <span class="hljs-built_in">ls</span>

<span class="hljs-comment"># Deploy a model from the catalog</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints catalog deploy --repo meta-llama/Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct

<span class="hljs-comment"># Deploy with a custom name</span>
<span class="hljs-meta">&gt;&gt;&gt; </span>hf endpoints catalog deploy --repo meta-llama/Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct --name my-llama-endpoint<!----></pre></div><!----> <a class="!text-gray-400 !no-underline text-sm flex items-center not-prose mt-4" href="https://github.com/huggingface/huggingface_hub/blob/main/docs/source/en/guides/cli.md" target="_blank"><svg class="mr-1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 32 32"><path d="M31,16l-7,7l-1.41-1.41L28.17,16l-5.58-5.59L24,9l7,7z"></path><path d="M1,16l7-7l1.41,1.41L3.83,16l5.58,5.59L8,23l-7-7z"></path><path d="M12.419,25.484L17.639,6.552l1.932,0.518L14.351,26.002z"></path></svg><!----> <span><span class="underline">Update</span> on GitHub</span></a><!----> <p></p><!--]--><!----><!--]--><!--]--><!--]--> <!--[-1--><!--]--><!--]-->
